  (function() {
    var jdgmThemeFixes = null;
    if (!jdgmThemeFixes) return;

    // "All themes" fix: CSS and JS applied on every theme. CSS is injected
    // before the per-theme fix so the theme-specific CSS wins the cascade.
    // HTML is never applied globally (too risky).
    var allThemesFix = jdgmThemeFixes['all'];
    if (allThemesFix && allThemesFix.css) {
      var allStyleTag = document.createElement('style');
      allStyleTag.classList.add('jdgm-theme-fix-style-all');

      document.head.append(allStyleTag);
    };

    if (allThemesFix && allThemesFix.js) {
      var allScriptTag = document.createElement('script');
      allScriptTag.classList.add('jdgm-theme-fix-script-all');
      allScriptTag.innerHTML = allThemesFix.js;
      document.head.append(allScriptTag);

    var thisThemeFix = jdgmThemeFixes[Shopify.theme.id];

        var htmlDiv = document.createElement('div');

        htmlDiv.innerHTML = thisThemeFix.html;
        document.body.append(htmlDiv);
      });

      styleTag.classList.add('jdgm-theme-fix-style');
      styleTag.innerHTML = thisThemeFix.css;
      document.head.append(styleTag);
    };

    if (thisThemeFix.js) {
      var scriptTag = document.createElement('script');
      scriptTag.classList.add('jdgm-theme-fix-script');
      scriptTag.innerHTML = thisThemeFix.js;
      document.head.append(scriptTag);

          <h2

          <h2
            data-block-id="heading_yGWAQr"

            About brand
          </h2>
        
        

          <div
            data-block-id="text_U49Wf9"
            class="hero__text  rte"
          >
            <p>Evanlite was founded in <strong>2017 in Poland </strong>and has been developing its wheels for nearly a decade in southern Poland - a region shaped by heavy industry and manufacturing. It’s a place where raw materials and engineering discipline have always mattered.</p><p>Evanlite belongs to the <strong>Lemonbike.eu</strong> company, which has been active in the bicycle industry since <strong>1997</strong>.</p>

            class="hero__text  rte"
          >
            <p>Behind Evanlite stands Lemonbike.eu — <strong>a company with over 25 years</strong> of experience in the bicycle industry. Over the years, Lemonbike has introduced many innovations. <br/>It was the first company to introduce carbon MTB wheels to the domestic market in the early 2010s, which are now an industry standard.</p><p>Evanlite brand was created as a result of many years of practice, testing, and constant improvement. We develop our products based on our own experience — as riders and as people who have lived with cycling for decades.</p>

    <div class="hero__overlay__content hero__overlay__content--left hero__overlay__content--mobile--left rte">
      
        

          <h2
            data-block-id="subheading_HLjejj"
            class="hero__subheading hero"
          >
            Brand Vision
          </h2>
        

          <h2
            data-block-id="heading_qwHU7H"
            class="hero__title hero"
          >
            Our Quality
          </h2>
        
        

          <div
            data-block-id="text_iEfnhD"
            class="hero__text  rte"
          >
            <p><strong>Every wheel is hand-built by us. Real people build it.</strong> It is not made on a factory line, but in a place where experience matters and every detail is important.</p><p>We choose components with care. Each wheelset is a balanced system - reliable hubs, strong spokes that keep tension, and rims that are light where possible and strong where needed. We know what works in hard terrain and what works best on fast, flat rides. We do not follow trends. <strong>We build wheels for real riding.</strong></p>
          </div>
        


      
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

</section><section id="shopify-section-sections--25646397555027__custom_liquid_eaYxrq" class="shopify-section shopify-section-group-footer-group spaced-section"><style>
  #custom-privacy-settings-wrapper {
    text-align: right!important;
    padding: 1% !important;
 
    width: 100% !important;
    
  }

  #custom-privacy-settings-wrapper #custom-shopify-privacy-policy-link {
    color: #ffffff !important;
    font-size: 0.8em !important;
    text-decoration: underline!important;
  }

  #shopify-privacy-banner h2, 
  [id*="shopify-privacy-banner"] h2 {
    font-size: 15px !important;
  }

  #shopify-privacy-banner h3, 
  [id*="shopify-privacy-banner"] h3 {
    font-size: 13px !important;
  }

  #shopify-privacy-banner strong,
  [id*="shopify-privacy-banner"] strong,
  #shopify-privacy-banner label,
  [id*="shopify-privacy-banner"] label {
    font-size: 11px !important;
  }

  #shopify-privacy-banner p, 
  [id*="shopify-privacy-banner"] p {
    font-size: 11px !important;
    line-height: 1.4 !important;
  }

  #shopify-privacy-banner button, 
  [id*="shopify-privacy-banner"] button {
    font-size: 11px !important;
    padding: 8px 12px !important;
  }
</style>

<div id="custom-privacy-settings-wrapper">
  <a href="#" id="custom-shopify-privacy-policy-link">
    Ustawienia - pliki cookies
  </a>
</div>

<script>
  (function() {
    const wrapper = document.getElementById('custom-privacy-settings-wrapper');
    const link = document.getElementById('custom-shopify-privacy-policy-link');
    
    if (wrapper) {
      const parentSection = wrapper.closest('.shopify-section');
      if (parentSection) {
        parentSection.style.setProperty('background', '#000000', 'important');
        parentSection.style.setProperty('background-color', '#000000', 'important');
        parentSection.style.setProperty('padding', '0', 'important');
        parentSection.style.setProperty('margin', '0', 'important');
      }
    }

    if (link) {
      link.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (typeof window.privacyBanner !== 'undefined' && typeof window.privacyBanner.showPreferences === 'function') {
          window.privacyBanner.showPreferences();
        } else if (typeof privacyBanner !== 'undefined' && typeof privacyBanner.showPreferences === 'function') {
          privacyBanner.showPreferences();
        }
      });
    }
  })();
</script>


<style>
  html {
    background-color: #408267 !important; /* kolor tego tła */
  }


  }
</style>


</section>

<script>
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    const textarea = document.getElementById('CartDrawer-note');
    const noteLink = document.querySelector('.giftnote__drawercart__addnote');

    textarea.addEventListener('input', function () {
      if (textarea.value.length > 0) {
        noteLink.innerHTML = "Edit order note";
      } else {
        noteLink.innerHTML = "Add order note";
      }
    });
  });
</script>

                    <li class="wt-language-list__item">

                        value="en"
                        
                          checked
                        
                      >

                        for="wt-lang_en"
                      >

                      </label>
                    </li>

            </form>

      };

      window.cartStrings = {
        error: `There was an error while updating your cart. Please try again.`,
        quantityError: `You can only add [quantity] of this item to your cart.`,

      window.variantStrings = {
        addToCart: `Add to cart`,
        soldOut: `Sold out`,
        unavailable: `Unavailable`,
        unavailable_with_option: `[value] [Unavailable]`,

      window.accessibilityStrings = {
